# Simulated trading session — 20 symbols, 24 h pre-historic + 12 h run (tactics default, signals on)

Real BingX 1m data, timeframe lanes 1 / 5 / 15 / 30 min set (independent + combined; traded: 1m, 1m+, 5m, 5m+, 15m, 15m+, 30m), strategies Normal, Trailing, Axis, signals. Balance $20.00; each order volume unit = 2.0 % of the realized equity at entry ($0.00–$0.46 before the caps) at 10×; 0.20 % round-trip cost on every close. Window 2026-10-07T00:00 → 2026-10-08T00:00 UTC. Engine: Base 2326/25098 pairs passed (incl. signal pairs) — per range, passed / evaluated pairs · median Base PF of the passed pairs: Wide 326/19072 · PF 1.53 · Micro 233/5900 · PF 2.25 · Minimal 1767/24972 · PF 1.79 · Short 632/8176 · PF 1.53 · General 489/8176 · PF 1.51 · Long 523/8176 · PF 1.50 · Signals 126/126 · PF 1.08; Main 2389 pairs, 433202 tapes, Real seats: 10596 engine configs + 3780 signal configs (every config of the active signals), compute 710 s. Causal: Base / Main / Real ranked on the history before the run.

**Result (as live sizes it, position cap 0.75× equity per symbol × side, gross cap 7× equity (x01 defaults (no live caps in the settings))):** balance $20.00 → $17.79 (-11.03 %, closed orders) · equity at end $15.32 (open at end: 40 positions / 9601 orders, MTM -$2.47 (exact: executed by the engine through every gate, cap and Block volume, marked to market)) · PF $ 0.78 (gross profit $ ÷ gross loss $ as sized) · PF unit 0.63 (every order at one unit: the engine's PF) · 100 positions / 45866 orders (incl. 4212 capped to $0) · WR 57.84 % · DDT (closed trades, $) 22.00 h · DDR – (net ≤ 0) · equity max drawdown $4.84 (24.01 %) · margin used max $14.32 · open avg 37.35 pos / 4629.30 orders (peak 40 / 8412)

**Caps:** position cap 0.75× equity per symbol × side, gross cap 7× equity (x01 defaults (no live caps in the settings)) — 4212 orders capped to $0, 41246 scaled down (open at end: 326 capped, 9268 scaled) · binding: position cap 38375, gross cap 54431. **Without the caps:** balance $20.00 → -$27.54 (-237.70 %) · PF $ 0.37 · equity at end -$32.54 · equity max drawdown $59.63 (282.11 %) · margin used max $250.33 · infeasible: margin exceeded equity for 1439 min.

## Base said, the book did — the same ranges on the same basis

The gates judge a set on its **unit** PF (per-order return, unsized). The headline result is in **dollars**, after
the live sizing and its caps. So a range has three numbers that must be read together, and only the first two are
on the same basis: the median unit PF of the sets Base passed, the unit PF those sets actually traded at, and the
dollar PF after sizing. "Base → book" is the second divided by the first: how much of the validated edge survived
out of sample. "sizing" is the third divided by the second: what the live caps did to it — above 1 they flattered
the range, below 1 they ate the edge. A row where Base is high and "Base → book" is low is selection, not sizing;
a row where "Base → book" is near 1 and "sizing" is far from it is the caps.

| range | Base passed (median PF unit) | traded PF unit | Base → book | traded PF $ | sizing | orders |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Micro | 2.25 | 0.26 | 0.12 | 0.50 | 1.91 | 97 |
| Minimal | 1.79 | 0.63 | 0.35 | 0.76 | 1.21 | 34584 |
| Short | 1.53 | 0.59 | 0.38 | 0.32 | 0.55 | 2509 |
| General | 1.51 | 0.76 | 0.50 | 1.23 | 1.61 | 728 |
| Long | 1.50 | 0.90 | 0.60 | 1.02 | 1.14 | 819 |
| Wide | 1.53 | 0.59 | 0.38 | 0.85 | 1.46 | 765 |
| Signals | 1.08 | 0.61 | 0.56 | 0.93 | 1.54 | 6364 |

### Seat evaluation (configEval) at the run start, per range (configs failing at each gate, first gate missed)

Each engine config on its own closes at the run start (configEval: the gates the seat selection applies — not the Base stage, which ranks bot × indication pairs): over the selection window (336 h) ≥ max(3, 12) closes, positive net, PF ≥ its range's minimum (stage 1.05; micro 1.05, minimal 1.05, short 1.05, general 1.05, long 1.05), drawdown time ≤ min(163.33 h, 35 h × span ÷ 72 h), span = the tape's own history inside the 336 h window (ddtLimitH: a 1m tape with 72 h of history → 35 h; a full window → 163.33 h), drawdown ratio ≤ 1, last 15 closes at the same PF / DDT, range cells' last 75 at PF ≥ 1.05, positive lower-confidence bound, green hours ≥ 50 %. Real entries then check the last 15 closes again. Signal configs are not evaluated here: they are seated by their own signal activation. PF medians: the configEval window PF of every evaluated config / of the passed ones (unit basis; a config with no loss counts at the engine's placeholder 4).
